# ERP UI Shell Screen Catalog

This catalog is the implementation inventory for the metadata-driven ERP UI shell. It is generated from the active registry metadata and records every preserved real screen, every UI-only screen definition, and every former-placeholder compatibility route.

## Scope summary

- Preserved existing real screens: **75**.
- Metadata-driven UI Shell screens: **524**.
- Former placeholder entries replaced: **52** (49 target screens; 41 additional compatibility route names).
- UI Shell GET routes registered by metadata: **2752**, plus **41** non-duplicate compatibility routes.
- Discoverable UI Shell and legacy compatibility permissions: **6377**.
- Persistence, database queries, writes, and business workflows: **not implemented by this shell**.

## Architecture and reading guide

- Each UI Shell resource is normalized by `ErpUiScreenBlueprints`, exposed by `ErpUiScreenRegistry`, rendered by `ErpUiShellController`, and registered after real module routes. A pre-existing route name always wins.
- Resource routes use `index`, `data`, `create`, `{doc_num}`, `{doc_num}/edit`, and `{doc_num}/clone` only when supported. Report/inquiry screens expose `index` and `data`. No persistence route exists.
- The `Permission` column records the view permission. Resource metadata also declares only the configured action permissions, including create/edit/clone/delete/trash/restore/approve/cancel/close/post/print/export/document-number control as appropriate.
- `Tabs`, `Main fields`, and `Repeaters` are taken from the actual normalized metadata. Field types appear in code style. A `>` inside repeaters denotes nesting.
- Existing real screens retain their module-owned controllers, forms, tabs, fields, repeaters, validation, and persistence. Their internals were deliberately not replaced with shell metadata.

## Former placeholder compatibility map

All former placeholder entries now resolve to a full metadata definition. If the modern screen already owns the exact route name, no duplicate alias is registered; otherwise the legacy GET route remains available under its original view permission.

## Implementation boundary

- Index tables use server-side DataTables configuration but return the exact empty payload: `draw`, `recordsTotal: 0`, `recordsFiltered: 0`, and `data: []`.
- Create, View, Edit, and Clone share the same metadata. The public `doc_num` is displayed only as neutral source context.
- Save/approve/post/delete and other operational buttons are presentation-only and show localized feedback; they never submit or mutate state.
- Card repeaters support add, duplicate, delete, reindexing, nested repeaters, Select2/date/Summernote reinitialization, and keyboard shortcuts without persistence.
- Arabic and English screen, field, action, status, empty-state, and permission-action labels are supplied by the registry metadata and `erp_ui_shell` translation files.
